{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1783622635840,"sessionID":"ses_0b7d1e8dbffeaY3e5VHOE6QC4T","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_00_w934WXhd6lKvaPd7Z1xp8791","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"content":"import pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.stats import chi2\n\n\ndef answer() -> list[int]:\n    df = pd.read_csv(\"/app/dataset/synthetic_stroke_data.csv\")\n    df.columns = df.columns.str.strip()\n\n    df[\"avg_glucose_level\"] = pd.to_numeric(df[\"avg_glucose_level\"], errors=\"coerce\")\n    df[\"bmi\"] = pd.to_numeric(df[\"bmi\"], errors=\"coerce\")\n    df[\"age\"] = pd.to_numeric(df[\"age\"], errors=\"coerce\")\n\n    # Step 1: Technical noise - values outside thresholds\n    tech_noise = set()\n    tech_noise.update(df[df[\"age\"] > 120].index.tolist())\n    tech_noise.update(df[(df[\"bmi\"] < 10) | (df[\"bmi\"] > 60)].index.tolist())\n    tech_noise.update(df[(df[\"avg_glucose_level\"] < 20) | (df[\"avg_glucose_level\"] > 800)].index.tolist())\n\n    # Work on clean data (no technical noise)\n    clean = df.drop(index=tech_noise).copy()\n\n    # Step 2: 3*IQR outlier detection\n    def find_iqr_outliers(series):\n        q1, q3 = series.quantianle(0.25), series.quantile(0.75)\n        iqr = q3 - q1\n        lo, hi = q1 - 3 * iqr, q3 + 3 * iqr\n        return (series < lo) | (series > hi)\n\n    iqr_age = find_iqr_outliers(clean[\"age\"])\n    iqr_bmi = find_iqr_outliers(clean[\"bmi\"])\n    iqr_glu = find_iqr_outliers(clean[\"avg_glucose_level\"])\n\n    any_iqr = iqr_age | iqr_bmi | iqr_glu\n    glu_out_idx = set(clean[iqr_glu].index)\n\n    # Step 3: Stroke association rule for avg_glucose_level outliers\n    excluded_glu = set()\n    for val in clean.loc[list(glu_out_idx), \"avg_glucose_level\"].unique():\n        mask = clean[\"avg_glucose_level\"] == val\n        rows = clean[mask]\n        sr = rows[\"stroke\"].mean()\n\n        if sr >= 0.5:\n            excluded_glu.update(rows.index)\n        elif sr > 0.2:\n            log2_glu = np.log2(clean[\"avg_glucose_level\"])\n            q1, q3 = log2_glu.quantile(0.25), log2_glu.quantile(0.75)\n            iqr = q3 - q1\n            lo, hi = q1 - 3 * iqr, q3 + 3 * iqr\n\n            these_log = log2_glu.loc[rows.index]\n            still_out = (these_log < lo) | (these_log > hi)\n            excluded_glu.update(rows.index[~still_out])\n\n            still_out_rows = clean.loc[rows.index[still_out]]\n            for lv in these_log[still_out].unique():\n                same_log_mask = log2_glu == lv\n                sr2 = clean.loc[same_log_mask, \"stroke\"].mean()\n                if sr2 >= 0.5:\n                    excluded_glu.update(clean[same_log_mask].index\n                                        & set(still_out_rows.index))\n\n    # Final IQR outliers (stroke-adjusted)\n    iqr_indices = (set(clean[any_iqr].index) - glu_out_idx) | (glu_out_idx - excluded_glu)\n\n    # Step 4: Remove IQR outliers for subsequent analysis\n    remaining = clean.drop(index=iqr_indices).copy()\n\n    # Step 5: Risk proxy (product of normalized numerical features)\n    risk_proxy = (\n        (remaining[\"age\"] / remaining[\"age\"].max())\n        * (remaining[\"bmi\"] / remaining[\"bmi\"].max())\n        * (remaining[\"avg_glucose_level\"] / remaining[\"avg_glucose_level\"].max())\n    )\n\n    # Step 6: Mahalanobis distance\n    X = remaining[[\"age\", \"bmi\", \"avg_glucose_level\"]].values\n    cov = np.cov(X, rowvar=False)\n    if np.linalg.cond(cov) > 1e12:\n        cov += np.eye(3) * 1e-8\n    inv_cov = np.linalg.inv(cov)\n    mean = X.mean(axis=0)\n    d2 = np.sum((X - mean) @ inv_cov * (X - mean), axis=1)\n\n    crit = chi2.ppf(0.99, df=3)\n    maha_idx = set(remaining[d2 > crit].index)\n\n    # Step 7: Categorical variable checks\n    cat = set()\n    cat.update(\n        df[\n            (df[\"work_type\"].str.strip().str.lower() == \"children\")\n            & (df[\"smoking_status\"].str.strip().str.lower().isin([\"smokes\", \"formerly smoked\"]))\n        ].index\n    )\n    cat.update(\n        df[(df[\"age\"] <= 3) & (df[\"work_type\"].str.strip().str.lower() == \"student\")].index\n    )\n\n    all_outliers = sorted(tech_noise | iqr_indices | maha_idx | cat)\n    return all_outliers\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import json\n    with open(\"/app/answer.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(answer(), f, default=list)\n","filePath":"/app/answer.py"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/app/answer.py","exists":true,"truncated":false},"title":"app/answer.py","time":{"start":1783622635822,"end":1783622635838}},"id":"prt_f48316f5c001dKzjo1EvfkWvs2","sessionID":"ses_0b7d1e8dbffeaY3e5VHOE6QC4T","messageID":"msg_f48314bd6001Z6aJwy3NZeylOe"}}
